## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目 GPU 白名单：westd-22162 cuda:0~3（4 张 RTX 6000 Pro）
- gsm8k_qw_rand_s030 刚失败，**不要盲目重跑**——必须等诊断 Worker 回调确认失败原因后再决定
- DS accuracy 数据已降为定性观察（D086），不再投入 GPU 做 DS 8k eval
- batch1 accuracy 数据正在 v2 rescore 修正中，引用 batch1 数据必须标注 "pre-v2" 或用修正后版本
- D081 决策：QW rand_s030 原本不重跑（DS 为主证据），后来 Director 改为需要跑 C10 对照组——如果再次失败需汇报 Director 决策是否放弃

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照

- Python 环境：需 Worker SSH 探查确认（aml-train 镜像用 conda base，路径 /root/miniconda3/bin/python）

  - gsm8k_qw_rand_s025 (PID 281019) — RUNNING，cron 监控中
  - acc_ds_s025_8k_v2 (PID 282534) — RUNNING，cron 监控中
- 日志路径模式：~/runs/phase-gated-compound-sparse/{exp_id}.log
- W&B project: phase-gated-compound-sparse（具体 entity 需从 config 确认）

### 3. 📊 当前状态
- **刚做完**：收到 gsm8k_qw_rand_s030 失败 CRON 回调，已派 Worker `worker_diagnose_gsm8k_qw_rand_s030_20260515T222521` 诊断失败原因
- **正在等**：诊断 Worker 回调（读日志判断 OOM/代码bug/其他）
- **RUNNING 实验**：
  - gsm8k_qw_rand_s025：C10 QW random s=0.25 对照组（正常运行）
  - acc_ds_s025_8k_v2：DS s025 accuracy eval 8k tokens
- **QUEUED/待 GPU**：acc_qw_s025_n100_b2_fix, acc_qw_s020_n100_b2_fix, acc_ds_s030_8k_v2, acc_ds_s015_8k_v2
- **未向 Director 汇报本次失败**（等诊断结果后一并汇报）

### 4. 📋 待办
- 诊断 Worker 回调到达 → 读失败原因 → update_registry 写 failure_reason → 汇报 Director（附修复方案或放弃建议）
- 如果是 OOM/简单 bug → 修复后重新提交 gsm8k_qw_rand_s030（需确认有空闲 GPU）
- 如果是系统性问题（与 s025 共享的代码 bug）→ 紧急通知 Director，s025 也可能受影响
- gsm8k_qw_rand_s025 完成回调 → 按 on-experiment-done 流程处理（C10 对照组数据）
- acc_ds_s025_8k_v2 完成回调 → 更新 dose curve
- 所有 C10 实验完成 → 编译完整 QW accuracy dose curve → Round 10 consistency check

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **核心结论**：importance-based eviction 的 compound interaction severity 随 sparsity 单调递增（n=500 confirmed）
- **截稿 2026-05-25（距今 10 天）**，C10 是最后缺失的实验组
- **C10 目的**：QW random masking 作为 importance masking 的对照组，证明 compound effect 不是 random pruning 的 artifact
- **gsm8k_profiling_qw 中 rand_s030 结果 INVALID**——这是重跑的原因。如果再次失败，Director 可能决定只用 s025 单点数据（D081 原本就倾向不重跑）
- **易错点**：G× 数值已从 58-241× 下修到 21-64×（D082），论文引用不要用旧数值；聚合方法已从 nanmean 改为 nanmedian + Wilcoxon（D083）

      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks.       "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task.     "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

The real issue is whether I need to re-run batch2 with 8k tokens for s015 and s030 to maintain consistency. <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01JZG6pQqDz9DQKNawqSxBmi`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

Looking at the roadmap, the batch1 v2 analysis and the full dose curve compilation are already being handled by the first worker, and once those finish I'll need to run the Round 10 consistency check. {
  "task_type": "research:round10_data_prep",
  "project": "phase-gated-compound-sparse",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：Round 10 数据编译 + 一致性检查准备\n\n### 背景\n项目 phase-gated-compound-sparse，截稿 2026-05-25（9 天）。38 个计划实验全部完成，正在做最后的数据编译和 Round 10 审稿准备。\n\n### 目标\n1. 从 registry 读取所有 DONE 实验的关键 metrics，编译成综合数据表\n2. 读取 paper_framing.md 了解论文 claims\n3. 交叉验证每个 claim 是否有充分实验支撑\n4. 标注数据一致性问题（如批次间差异、token budget 不匹配等）\n5. 产出 Round 10 consistency check 的 checklist\n\n### 具体步骤\n\n**Step 1: 读取核心文档**\n- `read_project_file(file=\"paper_framing.md\")` — 了解 claims\n- `read_experiment_plan_with_status()` — 了解每个 claim 的实验支撑\n- `read_project_file(file=\"ROADMAP.md\")` — 了解决策日志\n\n**Step 2: 编译数据表**\n\n用 `read_registry` 读取所有 DONE 实验，按 claim 分组编译：\n\nA) **II dose curve (DS, n=50)**\n| Sparsity | II_low_median | II_mid_median | II_high_median | Wilcoxon p |\n从 bf16_importance_s015/s020/s025/s030 提取\n\nB) **II dose curve (DS, n=500)**\n从 c2_n500_deepseek 提取\n\nC) **II dose curve (QW, n=50 + n=500)**\n从 bf16_qw_imp_s025/s030 和 c2_qw_n500_r3 提取\n\nD) **Random vs Importance 对比**\n从 bf16_ds_rand_s020/s025, v6_random_s025/s030 提取\n\nE) **GSM8K compound effect (C10)**\n从 gsm8k_profiling_ds, gsm8k_profiling_qw, gsm8k_qw_rand_s025_v2, gsm8k_qw_rand_s030_v2 提取\n\nF) **QW Accuracy dose curve (待更新)**\n标注：batch1 v2 rescore 正在分析中（另一个 Worker 处理），此处用已有数据占位\n\nG) **DS 8k Accuracy dose curve**\n从 acc_ds_dense_8k_v2, acc_ds_s015_8k_v2, acc_ds_s020_8k_v2, acc_ds_s025_8k_v2 提取\n标注 acc_ds_s030_8k_v2 仍在 RUNNING\n\n**Step 3: 一致性检查**\n- 论文中引用的所有数值是否与 registry 一致？\n- G× 数值是否使用下修后的 21-64×（不是旧的 58-241×）？\n- batch1 数据是否标注了 \"pre-v2\" 或已用 v2 修正值？\n- 聚合方法是否统一使用 nanmedian + Wilcoxon？\n- 每个 claim 是否有至少一个 primary 实验 + 一个 supporting 实验？\n- negative 结果（C4 Qwen entropy, C5b pilot, s3c DS s010）是否在 framing 中正确处理？\n\n**Step 4: 输出**\n将结果写入 `save_project_file(file=\"round10_data_compilation.md\")`\n\n格式：\n1. 数据总表（按 claim 分组）\n2. 一致性检查 checklist（✅/⚠️/❌）\n3. 待解决问题清单（阻塞 Round 10 的事项）\n4. 建议：是否已准备好进入 Round 10",
